Windows-based developer who uses Codex for crawler/emulator tooling, enterprise Leap repositories, MCP, browser/network diagnostics, Gitea deployments, and documentation workflows. Usually writes task requests in Chinese. Values exact evidence, preservation of existing state and user-controlled accounts, and a concrete verified outcome rather than a plan or generic troubleshooting. Works across checkout-specific projects, so cwd is a lead rather than proof of the true target. Uses user-maintained `sub2api` routes and custom API configuration alongside browser/OAuth workflows; keep those authentication paths distinct and do not alter a provider without explicit direction. For cross-platform work, expects a platform layer that does not damage the established runtime and clear labeling of simulated versus real-machine evidence.

## User preferences

- Make requested repair/configuration changes directly when authorized; keep them minimal and reversible, then verify the user-visible or persisted result. For user-facing documentation, confirm whether the target is a visible desktop archive rather than an internal runtime path.

- Before editing or deleting, inspect the authoritative path and current state. Preserve unrelated work, credentials, account actions, active services, login/crawler state, and explicitly protected data.
- At task completion, inspect the current project for unrelated intermediate artifacts; after cleanup approval, remove only clearly unnecessary task byproducts and prefer `.gitignore` coverage for repeatable ones. [ad-hoc note]
- When asked for real connectivity or "继续", perform the concrete end-to-end check and follow the visible blocker. Do not substitute an agent/backend transport check for the user's browser/profile route.
- Report partial, blocked, and unverified states precisely. HTTP 200, a listening port, formatter success, or a screenshot does not prove the requested end-to-end result.
- For evidence requests, return source-backed paths, URLs, logs, or lines; do not infer missing data. Separate historical state from a fresh live conclusion.
- For work-history documentation, make "我的做了什么" file/module-level and evidence-oriented; attribute ownership only to the confirmed Git identity and separate current test results, historical runs, and model-assisted review from stronger claims.
- For Chinese PPT/source-document requests, make the content "详细具体" enough for slide-by-slide reuse; use real run evidence and state data/safety boundaries.
- For a capture-console request, expose URL queue state, per-post stages, metrics, and artifact locations, but never identities, request payloads, or session material.
- Follow named templates/scope boundaries exactly. Modify the existing artifact in place when asked to continue; do not add custom sections or unrelated refactors.

## request_user_input availability

Use the `request_user_input` tool only when it is listed in the available tools for this turn.

In Default mode, strongly prefer making reasonable assumptions and executing the user's request rather than stopping to ask questions.

## Plugins
A plugin is a local bundle of skills, MCP servers, and apps.
### How to use plugins
- Skill naming: If a plugin contributes skills, those skill entries are prefixed with `plugin_name:` in the Skills list.
- MCP naming: Plugin-provided MCP tools keep standard MCP identifiers such as `mcp__server__tool`; use tool provenance to tell which plugin they come from.
- Trigger rules: If the user explicitly names a plugin, prefer capabilities associated with that plugin for that turn.
- Relationship to capabilities: Plugins are not invoked directly. Use their underlying skills, MCP tools, and app tools to help solve the task.
- Relevance: Determine what a plugin can help with from explicit user mention or from the plugin-associated skills, MCP tools, and apps exposed elsewhere in this turn.
- Missing/blocked: If the user requests a plugin that does not have relevant callable capabilities for the task, say so briefly and continue with the best fallback.
</plugins_instructions>

- macOS 当前只有启动和 transport 适配，明确标记 `deep_capture_runner: not-implemented-for-macos`。
- 代码状态不错：刚重跑核心 179 项测试，全部通过。
- 发现一个值得处理的遗留问题：[web_v2/index.html](C:/Users/lenovo/Desktop/facebook/web_v2/index.html) 不是有效 UTF-8，存在约 41 个替换字符；旧版 [web/index.html](C:/Users/lenovo/Desktop/facebook/web/index.html) 正常。
- 项目目录不是 Git 仓库，运行证据目录约 3.76 GB，后续维护、版本追踪和产物清理都比较困难。

整体判断：核心 Reaction 采集、完整性门禁、双 Worker 和证据链已经做得比较扎实；现在最需要收尾的是修复 v2 页面编码、统一最新交接文档、补齐未实测链接类型，以及在真实 Mac 上完成深抓验收。本次只读回顾，没有修改文件或启动模拟器。

**OpenAI Codex**

## 当前任务

用户要求继续完善 Facebook Reaction 抓取项目，重点解决：

1. 对方使用 macOS，确认按 Apple Silicon M4 适配。
2. Windows 端口不能硬编码到对方环境。
3. 新加坡直连 Facebook，不应强制 Clash/小火箭/7897。
4. 在已登录浏览器中寻找可实际查看点赞用户的 Facebook URL，并作为验收样本。
5. 尽量通过模拟测试、跨平台 CI 和真实浏览器观察把方案完善。

## 当前仓库与 Git 状态

工作目录：

```text
C:\Users\lenovo\Desktop\facebook
```

GitHub：

```text
https://github.com/taoduo0428/facebook-reaction-capture
```

当前 `main` 最新提交：

```text
fd30da5 Make macOS entrypoints executable after download
```

已推送到 GitHub，工作区此前已确认干净。

GitHub Actions 最新 `main` CI：

- Windows 通过
- macOS 通过
- 207 项测试通过
- macOS 跳过少量需要真实 Windows/设备的测试

## 已完成的重要改动

### macOS / M4

新增：

- `facebook_android_capture.py`
  - 独立 Python Android 采集执行器
  - 支持 ADB、mitmdump、直连/上游代理模式
  - 目标帖子严格验证
  - Reaction 列表安全点击
  - XML 滚动分页
  - 限流、来源不匹配、未终止分页、可见性受限均不会误报成功
  - 保存部分证据
  - 代理恢复和进程清理
  - PCAP 在 Mac 页面上被禁用，因为当前 Mac 执行器尚未实现 PCAP

- `facebook_macos_runtime.py`
  - macOS AVD 启动

### 网络与端口

新增：

- `facebook_network.py`
- `test_facebook_network.py`

支持：

```json
"network": {
  "mode": "direct",
  "upstream_url": ""
}
```

直连模式：

- 默认模式
- 不要求 Clash
- 不要求 7897
- mitmproxy 使用 regular mode

上游模式：

```json
"network": {
  "mode": "upstream",
  "upstream_url": "http://127.0.0.1:7897"
}
```

兼容策略：

- 新配置默认 direct
- 已有 Windows 配置如果没有 `network` 字段，保留旧的 `http://127.0.0.1:7897` 行为
- 禁止代理 URL 中嵌入凭据
- 端口首次初始化会跳过占用端口
- 不杀未知进程
- API、Worker capture、native 端口分离

新增 API：

```text
GET /v1/setup
GET /v1/capabilities
```

页面会：

- 检查本机 SDK、ADB、AVD、依赖、网络配置
- Mac 自动禁用 PCAP checkbox
- 提示名单总数不代表当前账号能看到完整用户名单
- 提供 README 配置指南链接

Windows-based developer who uses Codex for crawler/emulator tooling, enterprise Leap repositories, MCP, browser/network diagnostics, Gitea deployments, and documentation workflows. Usually writes task requests in Chinese. Values exact evidence, preservation of existing state and user-controlled accounts, and a concrete verified outcome rather than a plan or generic troubleshooting. Works across checkout-specific projects, so cwd is a lead rather than proof of the true target. Uses user-maintained `sub2api` routes and custom API configuration alongside browser/OAuth workflows; keep those authentication paths distinct and do not alter a provider without explicit direction. For cross-platform work, expects a platform layer that does not damage the established runtime and clear labeling of simulated versus real-machine evidence.

- literature-review: Conduct comprehensive, systematic literature reviews using multiple academic databases (PubMed, arXiv, bioRxiv, Semantic Scholar, etc.). This skill should be used when conducting systematic literature reviews, meta-analyses, research synthesis, or comprehensive literature searches across biomedical, scientific, and technical domains. Creates professionally formatted markdown documents and PDFs with verified citations in multiple citation styles (APA, Nature, Vancouver, etc.). (file: r1/literature-review/SKILL.md)
- matplotlib: Low-level plotting library for full customization. Use when you need fine-grained control over every plot element, creating novel plot types, or integrating with specific scientific workflows. Export to PNG/PDF/SVG for publication. For quick statistical plots use seaborn; for interactive plots use plotly; for publication-ready multi-panel figures with journal styling, use scientific-visualization. (file: r1/matplotlib/SKILL.md)
- nature-paper2ppt: Build a complete but efficient Nature-style Chinese PPTX presentation from a scientific paper, preprint, PDF, article text, abstract, figure legends, or reading notes. Use this skill whenever the user asks to make slides/PPT/PPTX for journal club, group meeting, paper sharing, thesis seminar, lab meeting, department report, or academic presentation from a research paper, not only medical papers. It identifies the paper type and argument, selects only the figures needed for the story, writes Chinese slide content and speaker notes, creates the actual .pptx deck, and performs lightweight verification with cross-platform Python tooling by default. (file: r0/nature-paper2ppt/SKILL.md)

Applicable to social networks, biological networks, transportation systems, citation networks, and any domain involving pairwise relationships. (file: r1/networkx/SKILL.md)
- paper-lookup: Search 10 academic paper databases via REST APIs for research papers, preprints, and scholarly articles. Covers PubMed, PMC (full text), bioRxiv, medRxiv, arXiv, OpenAlex, Crossref, Semantic Scholar, CORE, Unpaywall. Use when searching for papers, citations, DOI/PMID lookups, abstracts, full text, open access, preprints, citation graphs, author search, or any scholarly literature query. Triggers on mentions of any supported database or requests like "find papers on X" or "look up this DOI". (file: r1/paper-lookup/SKILL.md)

- presentations:Presentations: Read, create or edit PowerPoint or Google Slides decks. Use for presentation, slide deck, PowerPoint, PPT, PPTX, or Google Slides requests. (file: r4/presentations/26.905.11957/skills/presentations/SKILL.md)

- scientific-visualization: Meta-skill for publication-ready figures. - scientific-writing: Core skill for the deep research and writing tool. Write scientific manuscripts in full paragraphs (never bullet points). IMRAD structure, citations (APA/AMA/Vancouver), figures/tables, reporting guidelines (CONSORT/STROBE/PRISMA), for research papers and journal submissions. (file: r1/scientific-writing/SKILL.md)
- scikit-learn: Machine learning in Python with scikit-learn. - shap: Model interpretability and explainability using SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations). **User**

这个mac电脑有没有模拟机？来测试

---


**OpenAI Codex**

有，Mac 可以使用 **Android Emulator（安卓模拟器）** 测试，M4 建议使用原生 ARM64 镜像。

推荐配置：

- 安装 Android Studio
- 安装 Android 35、Android Emulator、Platform Tools
- 创建 `arm64-v8a` 的 AVD
- 启动后在模拟器内手动安装 Facebook 并登录
- 使用仓库中的 Mac 检查命令验证：

```bash
./CHECK.command
./.venv/bin/python macos/prepare_device.py --serial emulator-5554
```

也可以手动创建：

```bash
sdkmanager "platform-tools" "emulator" \
  "platforms;android-35" \
  "system-images;android-35;google_apis;arm64-v8a"

avdmanager create avd \
  -n fb-m4-api35 \
  -k "system-images;android-35;google_apis;arm64-v8a"

emulator -avd fb-m4-api35 -port 5554
```

注意：这是 Android 模拟器，适合测试当前项目；iOS Simulator 不能运行 Android 版 Facebook，也不能替代本项目的 Android 测试链路。

---


**User**

我意思是有没有模拟mac

---


**OpenAI Codex**

明白了，你问的是“在 Windows 上模拟一台 Mac”。

目前没有适合本项目、可靠的“Mac 模拟器”可以在 Windows 上真实模拟 M4 macOS 环境。常见方案只有：

- **macOS 虚拟机**：需要 Apple 硬件授权环境，Windows 上通常不适合作为正式测试方案。
- **GitHub macOS Runner**：只能做 CI 和 Python/macOS 兼容性测试，不能验证 M4、Android 模拟器、Facebook 登录和证书链路。
- **Docker/WSL**：不能模拟 macOS，也不能证明 Mac 图形和 Android 链路可用。

因此可以在 Windows 上模拟测试：

- 直连/代理配置
- 动态端口
- ADB 命令
- ARM64 配置解析
- 分页、限流、结果完整性
- macOS 脚本的静态和单元测试

但不能用 Windows 模拟出真实 M4 Mac。最终仍需要真实 Mac，或者让对方提供远程 Mac 环境进行验收。

**OpenAI Codex**

已完成并推送到 GitHub：

[taoduo0428/facebook-reaction-capture](https://github.com/taoduo0428/facebook-reaction-capture)

最新提交：

```text
d8f9864 Fix portable source export on macOS temp paths
```

这次主要优化：

- 新增 **CONFIGURE.command**：Mac 双击即可进入一键配置流程。
- 自动检查/安装 Android SDK、Android 35 ARM64 镜像和 AVD。
- 自动避让 API、模拟器控制端口和 ADB 端口。
- 默认新加坡直连，不需要 Clash、小火箭或固定 `7897`。
- 自动配置 Facebook 链接始终由 `com.facebook.katana` 打开。
- 自动校验 Frida 官方 ARM64 文件 SHA-256。
- 自动配置 CA、Frida 和设备锁。
- 已有 AVD、账号、登录状态和本机配置不会覆盖。
- Windows 多链接任务默认复用已有 Facebook 进程，不再每条链接强制关闭重开；只有 Facebook 未运行或显式使用 `-ColdStartFacebook` 时才冷启动。
- 每条帖子仍独立保存结果并重新校验来源，避免数据串帖。
- 源码 ZIP 保留 Mac 脚本执行权限，解压后可以直接双击。

对方 Mac 的操作方式：

1. 安装 Python 3.12/3.13、Android Studio。
2. 在 Android Studio 的 SDK Manager 安装 **Android SDK Command-line Tools (latest)**。
3. 把合法取得的 Facebook ARM64 APK 放入仓库的 `setup-input` 文件夹。
4. 双击 `CONFIGURE.command`。
5. 配置完成后，在 Android 模拟器内手动登录 Facebook。
6. 双击 `START.command`，页面中每行粘贴一个帖子 URL。

验证结果：

- 本地发布门禁：222 项通过，1 项 macOS 专用锁测试在 Windows 跳过。
- GitHub Actions：Windows 通过，macOS 通过。
- 源码导出：82 个文件，未包含本机配置、APK、Cookie、证书私钥或账号状态。
- 工作区干净，远端 `main` 已确认指向 `d8f9864`。

真实 M4 的 Android 模拟器、Facebook 登录和完整 Reaction 分页仍需要对方实际运行 `CONFIGURE.command` 后验收。

**OpenAI Codex**

## 当前任务

继续完善并交付 Facebook Reaction 抓取项目，目标是让对方在 Apple Silicon Mac（M4）上可低门槛运行，适配新加坡直连网络，支持多个 Facebook 帖子 URL，并减少重复启动 Facebook 的时间开销。

工作目录：

```text
C:\Users\lenovo\Desktop\facebook
```

GitHub：

```text
https://github.com/taoduo0428/facebook-reaction-capture
```

## 已完成并已推送

远端 `main` 最新提交：

```text
d8f9864 Fix portable source export on macOS temp paths
```

最近重要提交：

- `6471ac6 Add one-click Mac provisioning and reuse Facebook across captures`
- `d8f9864 Fix portable source export on macOS temp paths`

GitHub Actions 最后一次已验证：

- Windows：通过
- macOS：通过
- 测试：222 项通过，1 项 macOS 专用 flock 测试在 Windows 跳过
- 源码导出：通过

本地最后确认：

- 工作区干净
- 交付 ZIP 已生成，82 个文件
- ZIP 不包含 `facebook.local.json`、`facebook_workers.json`、本机 setup 配置、APK、Cookie、PEM、MITM 文件等敏感内容

## 已实现的功能

Mac 一键入口：

```text
CONFIGURE.command
```

它会：

- 创建本机配置；
- 默认使用 `network.mode: direct`；
- 检查/安装 Android SDK、Android 35 Google APIs ARM64 镜像；
- 只创建缺失的专用 AVD，不覆盖已有 AVD 或账号数据；
- 自动避让 API、模拟器控制和 ADB 端口；
- 配置 Android CA；
- 下载并校验官方 Frida Android ARM64 文件；
- 安装合法取得的 Facebook APK；
- 设置 Facebook 链接由 `com.facebook.katana` 打开；
- 使用与采集器一致的 per-serial lock，避免配置过程打断正在运行的采集任务；
- 失败时暂停终端，保留错误提示。

默认 Frida 17.17.0 官方 ARM64 文件已实际下载验证：

```text
sha256:
09d1fad867b27d69562a79289f4c412e85867f5d38ab72877036ed35e4223021
```

Windows 多链接优化：

- `run_facebook_capture.ps1` 默认复用已有 Facebook 主进程；
- Facebook 不存在时才启动；
- 增加 `-ColdStartFacebook`，别名 `-ColdStart`，用于显式冷启动恢复；
- 每个帖子仍重新建立采集边界、独立输出和目标来源校验；
- 保留分页终止、限流冷却和完整性校验。

源码导出优化：

- `.command` 文件按 LF 保存；
- ZIP 中 Mac shell 入口保留 `100755` 执行权限；
- 修复 macOS 临时目录 symlink 导致的源码导出测试失败。

## 关键文档

- [README.md](C:\Users\lenovo\Desktop\facebook\README.md)
- [macos/QUICKSTART.md](C:\Users\lenovo\Desktop\facebook\macos\QUICKSTART.md)
- [FACEBOOK_BROWSER_SAMPLES.md](C:\Users\lenovo\Desktop\facebook\FACEBOOK_BROWSER_SAMPLES.md)
- [FACEBOOK_LINK_SUPPORT.md](C:\Users\lenovo\Desktop\facebook\FACEBOOK_LINK_SUPPORT.md)
- [macos/README.md](C:\Users\lenovo\Desktop\facebook\macos\README.md)

Mac 推荐流程：

```bash
./CONFIGURE.command
./START.command
```

用户需要：

1. 安装 Python 3.12/3.13 和 Android Studio；
2. 在 Android Studio SDK Manager 安装 Android SDK Command-line Tools；
3. 把合法取得的 Facebook ARM64 APK 放入 `setup-input/`；
4. 双击 `CONFIGURE.command`；
5. 在 Android 模拟器中手动登录 Facebook；
6. 双击 `START.command`；
7. 输入帖子 URL 测试。

新加坡直连不需要 Clash、小火箭或固定 `7897`。旧 macOS transport 适配层仍记录固定 `7897`，但文档已明确它是旧兼容入口；新部署使用 `CONFIGURE.command`。

## 浏览器样本

已通过用户登录的 Microsoft Edge Facebook 页面确认过：

正例：

```text
https://www.facebook.com/watch/?v=1069930052107630
```

- Japan Experience 视频帖
- 曾观察到 All 334、Like 279
- Like 名单可显示多条真实用户条目

```text
https://www.facebook.com/groups/1020765891821918/posts/2158908574674305/
```

- Chinese and South East Asian Cooking 群组帖
- 曾观察到 All 266、Like 228
- “See who reacted” 中可看到多条公开用户条目

受限负例：

```text
https://www.facebook.com/scmp/posts/pfbid02cwtTB6SMNxjqvbB8BhfET3KQrJZQSjxnbzxfE1LZXMT2EtN3vPK78zvvJVnPVe9il
```

```text
https://www.facebook.com/chinadaily/posts/pfbid0BLXGZ4puu1o5igYonBkhW3Zo1U1LqgELJhoU9iWorfhYuTSdvbck6c1sM2jKoHZVl
```

这两个页面只显示已点赞好友姓名，不能把总数当作完整用户名单。

## 当前状态与阻塞

真实 Apple Silicon M4 设备从未提供运行输出，因此以下内容仍未真实验收：

- Mac 上 Android Studio/SDK 实际安装；
- ARM64 AVD 启动；
- Facebook APK 安装和手动登录；
- Android CA/Conscrypt/app namespace；
- Frida server 实际连接；
- Mac 真实 Reaction 分页；
- Mac 真实 Facebook URL 类型兼容性。

目标此前已经多次因同一条件标记为 `blocked`。最近用户只是重复原目标，没有提供 M4 日志，因此没有继续修改代码。

恢复时应优先要求对方在仓库目录运行：

```bash
./CHECK.command
./.venv/bin/python macos/prepare_device.py --serial emulator-5554
```

然后提供：

- 终端输出；
- `CONFIGURE.command` 输出；
- 一次正例 URL 的实际抓取结果；
- 脱敏后的 `api-jobs/` 日志。

不要要求或接收 Cookie、CA 私钥、模拟器磁盘或账号状态。

## 重要约束

- 不修改用户现有 Windows 账号、AVD、Worker 注册表、历史采集数据；
- 不杀未知端口进程；
- 不把浏览器点赞总数当成完整用户名单；
- 不把 Windows 或 GitHub macOS runner 当成真实 M4 验收；
- 不上传账号、Cookie、代理凭据、私钥、APK 或模拟器数据；
- 保持 `sub2api` provider 不变；
- 用户偏好简体中文、直接执行、提供实际证据；
- 当前任务没有必要继续重复相同状态汇报；只有收到新的实机输出或发现新的仓库状态变化时才继续工作。

**OpenAI Codex**

<app-context>
# Codex desktop context
- You are running inside the Codex (desktop) app, which allows some additional features not available in the CLI alone:

### Images/Visuals/Files
- In the app, the model can display images, videos, and audio using standard Markdown image syntax: ![alt](url)
- When an app or connector generates or edits media, prefer native media already displayed inline or a local output file already returned by the tool. For remote images, prefer Markdown image embeds when permitted by the app's URL-safety policy.
- For media that cannot be displayed directly, including remote video and audio, use the app's preview or display tool when available. Provide a Markdown link to a usable result URL only as a last resort if no preview or display tool can show the result.
- Do not download remote media to work around display restrictions.
- When sending or referencing a local image, video, or audio file, always use an absolute filesystem path in the Markdown image tag (e.g., ![alt](/absolute/path.png)); relative paths and plain text will not render the media.
- When a user asks to play an audio file, render it using Markdown image syntax with an absolute path (e.g., ![audio](/absolute/path.mp3)).
- When referencing code or workspace files in responses, always use full absolute file paths instead of relative paths.
- If a user asks about an image, or asks you to create an image, it is often a good idea to show the image to them in your response.
- Return web URLs as Markdown links (e.g., [label](https://example.com)).

### Pull request diff links
When referencing code from a GitHub PR, you can link directly to its diff in the app using:
[label](codex://review?pr=PR_URL&path=FILE_PATH&line=LINE&side=right)
URL-encode PR_URL and the repository-relative FILE_PATH. Use a verified one-based LINE from the current PR diff. Use side=left for the original code or side=right for the updated code. Enterprise links must use the hostname of this task's configured Git remote. Use ordinary file links for workspace code.

### Workspace Dependencies
- For sheets, slides, and documents, call `load_workspace_dependencies` to find the bundled runtime and libraries.

### Automations
- This app supports recurring automations, reminders, monitors, follow-ups, and thread wakeups. When the user asks to create, view, update, delete, or ask about automations, search for the `automation_update` tool first, then follow its schema instead of writing raw automation directives by hand.
- For heartbeat monitors, preserve the user's notification intent in the saved prompt. Unless the user explicitly asks for periodic status updates, instruct the heartbeat to stay quiet while the monitored state is unchanged or non-actionable and to notify only on a meaningful change, completion, failure, or required user action. Do not add instructions such as "leave a brief status update" on every run.
- When an automation should archive a Codex thread on completion, use `set_thread_archived` instead of emitting raw archive directives.

### Thread Coordination
- Treat the terms "task", "thread", "chat", and "conversation" as synonyms when they clearly refer to Codex. Tool names use the term "thread" and Codex uses "task" in the UI. When providing user-facing responses, use "task".
- When the user asks to create, fork, inspect, continue, hand off, pin, archive, unarchive, rename, or otherwise manage Codex threads, search for the relevant thread tool first: `create_thread`, `fork_thread`, `list_threads`, `list_archived_threads`, `read_thread`, `wait_threads`, `send_message_to_thread`, `handoff_thread`, `set_thread_pinned`, `set_thread_archived`, or `set_thread_title`.
- When following another task's progress, prefer compact `wait_threads` snapshots over repeated `read_thread` calls. Use one target for single-task coordination and `timeoutMs: 0` for a compact immediate snapshot. `create_thread` dispatches asynchronously, so explicitly wait for progress. Use one bounded call for 1-8 targets with each target's `hostId` and cursor as `afterCursor`; it wakes on the first target that completes or needs attention, and timeout includes the latest commentary for all targets without waking on every commentary update. An up-to-date cursor suppresses already-delivered final text. Separate waits from one task may run serially. Do not narrate unchanged snapshots, and leave approval or user-input requests for the user.
- Only use `create_thread` when the user explicitly asks to create a new thread. Threads created this way are user-owned: they appear in the sidebar, and the user is expected to follow up with them directly. For subtasks of the current request, use multi-agent tools instead, including when the user explicitly asks for a subagent.
- After a successful `create_thread` call, emit `::created-thread{threadId="..."}` for a created thread or `::created-thread{clientThreadId="..."}` for queued worktree setup on its own line in your final response.

### Inline Code Comments
- Use the ::code-comment{...} directive when you need to attach feedback directly to specific code lines.
- Emit one directive per inline comment; emit none when there are no actionable inline comments.
- Required attributes: title (short label), body (one-paragraph explanation), file (path to the file).
- Optional attributes: start, end (1-based line numbers), priority (0-3).
- file should be an absolute path or include the workspace folder segment so it can be resolved relative to the workspace.
- Keep line ranges tight; end defaults to start.
- Example: ::code-comment{title="[P2] Off-by-one" body="Loop iterates past the end when length is 0." file="/path/to/foo.ts" start=10 end=11 priority=2}

### Git
- Branch prefix: `codex/`. Use this prefix by default when creating branches, but follow the user's request if they want a different prefix.

Decision boundary: should you use memory for a new user query?

  workspace history, conventions, or prior decisions.
- Hard skip examples: current time/date, simple translation, simple sentence
  rewrite, one-line shell command, trivial formatting.

  - the query mentions workspace/repo/module/path/files in MEMORY_SUMMARY below,
  - the user asks for prior context / consistency / previous decisions,
  - the task is ambiguous and could depend on earlier project choices,
  - the ask is a non-trivial and related to MEMORY_SUMMARY below.
- If unsure, do a quick memory pass.

Quick-pass budget:

During execution: if you hit repeated errors, confusing behavior, or suspect
relevant prior context, redo the quick memory pass.

How to decide whether to verify memory:

- Consider both risk of drift and verification effort.
- If a fact is likely to drift and is cheap to verify, verify it before
  answering.
- If a fact is likely to drift but verification is expensive, slow, or
  disruptive, it is acceptable to answer from memory in an interactive turn,
  but you should say that it is memory-derived, note that it may be stale, and
  consider offering to refresh it live.
- If a fact is lower-drift and expensive to verify, it is usually fine to
  answer from memory directly.

When answering from memory without current verification:

- If you rely on memory for a fact that you did not verify in the current turn,
  say so briefly in the final answer.
- If that fact is plausibly drift-prone or comes from an older note, older
  snapshot, or prior run summary, say that it may be stale or outdated.
- If live verification was skipped and a refresh would be useful in the
  interactive context, consider offering to verify or refresh it live.
- Do not present unverified memory-derived facts as confirmed-current.
- Prefer a short refresh offer for interactive questions, especially about prior
  results, commands, timing, or older snapshots.

  - do not include file paths or notes in this section
  - For every `citation_entries`, try to find and cite the corresponding rollout id if possible

Windows-based developer who uses Codex for crawler/emulator tooling, enterprise Leap repositories, MCP, browser/network diagnostics, Gitea deployments, and documentation workflows. Usually writes task requests in Chinese. Values exact evidence, preservation of existing state and user-controlled accounts, and a concrete verified outcome rather than a plan or generic troubleshooting. Works across checkout-specific projects, so cwd is a lead rather than proof of the true target. Uses user-maintained `sub2api` routes and custom API configuration alongside browser/OAuth workflows; keep those authentication paths distinct and do not alter a provider without explicit direction. For cross-platform work, expects a platform layer that does not damage the established runtime and clear labeling of simulated versus real-machine evidence.

## User preferences

- At task completion, inspect the current project for unrelated intermediate artifacts; after cleanup approval, remove only clearly unnecessary task byproducts and prefer `.gitignore` coverage for repeatable ones. [ad-hoc note]

Best for econometrics, time series, rigorous inference with coefficient tables. For guided statistical test selection with APA reporting use statistical-analysis. (file: r1/statsmodels/SKILL.md)
- study-abroad-advisor: Use when planning or preparing study-abroad applications, including graduate or taught-master school selection, program-fit analysis, CV/resume, SOP/PS, recommendation strategy, application portfolio, interview prep, offer comparison, timelines, or cross-document consistency checks. (file: r0/study-abroad-advisor/SKILL.md)
- superpowers:systematic-debugging: Use when encountering any bug, test failure, or unexpected behavior, before proposing fixes (file: r1/superpowers/systematic-debugging/SKILL.md)
- superpowers:verification-before-completion: Use when about to claim work is complete, fixed, or passing, before committing or creating PRs - requires running verification commands and confirming output before making any success claims; evidence before assertions always (file: r1/superpowers/verification-before-completion/SKILL.md)
- superpowers:writing-plans: Use when you have a spec or requirements for a multi-step task, before touching code (file: r1/superpowers/writing-plans/SKILL.md)
- template-creator:template-creator: Create or update a reusable personal Codex artifact-template skill. Use when the user invokes $template-creator or asks in natural language to create a reusable template from a reference document, presentation, spreadsheet, Google Docs, Slides, or Sheets link, ImageGen or Product Design image, email, Slack message, or Site project, or explicitly asks to edit or update a passed artifact-template skill. Do not use for one-off creation from an existing template. (file: r4/template-creator/26.905.11957/skills/template-creator/SKILL.md)
- visualize:visualize: Create visualizations and interactive tools directly in conversation. Proactively use to show how something works; explore 'what happens when', 'what changes', or 'help me understand'; compare or inspect; create simulations, maps, charts, graphs, and mockups. Use standard tools for static scientific figures. (file: r3/visualize/1.0.29/skills/visualize/SKILL.md)

仓库已经更新并推送成功：

- GitHub：[facebook-reaction-capture](https://github.com/taoduo0428/facebook-reaction-capture)
- 本地与远端 `main` 一致：`d8f9864`
- `CONFIGURE.command` 和 `START.command` 已提交为可执行文件
- 默认新加坡直连，不需要 Clash/小火箭
- 多个链接复用同一个 Facebook 模拟器和已启动进程
- 只有 Facebook 未运行或故障时才重新启动
- 端口首次配置会自动避让冲突

对方 Mac 上的使用方式：

```bash
./CONFIGURE.command
```

配置完成后登录 Facebook，再运行：

```bash
./START.command
```

网页中每行输入一个 Facebook 帖子 URL 即可批量抓取。

当前本机测试命令因环境没有安装 `pytest` 未执行成功；GitHub 远端此前的 Windows、macOS 和 222 项测试已通过。

If the evidence is incomplete, weak, indirect, merely consistent with completion, or leaves any requirement missing, incomplete, or unverified, keep working instead of marking the goal complete. If the objective is achieved, call update_goal with status "complete" so usage accounting is preserved. If the achieved goal has a token budget, report the final consumed token budget to the user after update_goal succeeds.

Blocked audit:
- Do not call update_goal with status "blocked" the first time a blocker appears.
- Only use status "blocked" when the same blocking condition has repeated for at least three consecutive goal turns, counting the original/user-triggered turn and any automatic goal continuations.
- If the user resumes a goal that was previously marked "blocked", treat the resumed run as a fresh blocked audit. If the same blocking condition then repeats for at least three consecutive resumed goal turns, call update_goal with status "blocked" again.
- Use status "blocked" only when you are truly at an impasse and cannot make meaningful progress without user input or an external-state change.
- Once the blocked threshold is satisfied, do not keep reporting that you are still blocked while leaving the goal active; call update_goal with status "blocked".
- Never use status "blocked" merely because the work is hard, slow, uncertain, incomplete, or would benefit from clarification.

Do not call update_goal unless the goal is complete or the strict blocked audit above is satisfied. Do not mark a goal complete merely because the budget is nearly exhausted or because you are stopping work.

**OpenAI Codex**

---


**OpenAI Codex**

初步结论已经能分开：第五个群组并不是“完全没写入口定位”，代码里有专门的 group-permalink 分支和安全上滑限制；第四个 Watch 则连 URL 特异性判断都没有把 `watch/?v=` 当成可直接验证目标。现在我对照失败运行的 UI XML，确认群组究竟缺的是哪一种控件标签。

---


**OpenAI Codex**

根因已经确认了：

- Watch：确实没有做完整的 `watch/?v=` 专用目标入口。当前严格 URL 判断只覆盖 `/posts/`、`story.php`、`/reel/`、`/videos/` 等，所以还没走到 Reaction 定位就被拦住。
- 群组：做过专用入口，但安全条件只覆盖“单张大图”的群组帖子；这次样本是多图网格，缺少代码要求的 `Photo/照片` 大图节点，因此程序拒绝上滑，Reaction 定位根本没机会执行。
- `pfbid`：你的判断对，必须换一个本账号能看到完整名单的正例，才能判断是 URL 形态问题还是帖子隐私问题。

我已经从刚才成功的 `share/p` 帖子找到了同一帖的直接 `pfbid` 地址，现在直接探测它来隔离这两个变量。

---


**OpenAI Codex**

这个正例把问题分得更清楚了：直接 `pfbid` URL 已经通过严格目标确认，说明它不是隐私受限；但进入 Android 后仍没找到 Reaction 数字。为了避免把“入口定位问题”说得太笼统，我再看一下这次验证报告是否已经解析出了可重开的数字帖子 ID。

---


**OpenAI Codex**

对，准确判断是：

- **第四个 Watch**：之前只做了少量解析支持，没有完成 `watch/?v=` 的严格目标入口。它在 Reaction 定位之前就失败了，因为 [run_facebook_capture.ps1](C:/Users/lenovo/Desktop/facebook/run_facebook_capture.ps1:335) 没把 `?v=` 识别为可验证目标。
- **第五个群组帖子**：做过专用入口，但只覆盖了“单张大图”布局。这次是多图网格，未满足 [explore_facebook_readonly.ps1](C:/Users/lenovo/Desktop/facebook/explore_facebook_readonly.ps1:674) 的安全结构条件，所以没有执行上滑和 Reaction 定位。
- **最后一个 `pfbid`**：受隐私限制的 SCMP 帖只能作为负例，不能用来判断 `pfbid` 是否支持。确实应该先找一个名单可见的正例。

我已经找到并测试了可见的 `pfbid` 正例：

```text
https://www.facebook.com/ATCTPE/posts/pfbid02qYWESeXuh3yj4oH6jHoy6xNKJLg8L777N5fFmjbZYNChhJ45ZE9S7CKtqKicdT7rl
```

它与成功的 `share/p/1DVLR4FNo6/` 是同一帖子：

- 浏览器可见 949 个 Reaction，并能打开用户列表。
- `share/p` 形式首屏成功抓到 26 个用户。
- 直接 `pfbid` 形式能确认目标，但没有找到 Reaction 入口。

这证明直接 `pfbid` 的失败不是隐私造成的。验证器只匹配到了 `pfbid` 字符串，没有把它绑定到已经出现在响应中的数字帖子 ID `1691321539667513`，所以后续没有按稳定的数字帖子地址重新打开。

因此需要修的三个点很明确：Watch URL 规范化、群组多图布局入口、`pfbid → 数字帖子 ID → 重新打开`。前面误触的 Reel 点赞仍保持现状，我没有再操作。
